current_state: The source tree now defaults normal `/generate` image generation to Gemini. The frontend uses `VITE_GENERATION_PROVIDER=gemini`, shows `Geminiで生成`, and sends `generationProvider: gemini`; the production `generate-image` Edge Function has been redeployed with the Gemini path and defaults to Gemini, enforces auth, brand editor role, feature allowlist, input validation, legal-safety rights confirmation, quota/rate limits, then calls server-side `_shared/geminiImage.ts`. Gemini requires `GEMINI_API_KEY` only in server-side runtime secrets and fails closed as `gemini_api_key_missing` / `gemini_image_request_failed` when the key is absent or invalid. Runway MCP/local-worker remains an explicit fallback/legacy path for `generationProvider=runway` or `VITE_GENERATION_PROVIDER=local-runway`, with the existing approved-client handoff/import worker retained for historical proofs and deliberate Runway recovery. H602 billing-readiness groundwork restores monthly quota enforcement and keeps Apple sandbox/tester no-real-charge verification as human/operator-gated work; migration `20260701143000_roll_free_subscription_period_for_usage_reservation.sql` is applied to production so active free-plan brands roll across month boundaries without enabling checkout or payment. Live Gemini production generation now reaches Google Generative Language with the active server-side secret and default Nano Banana model `gemini-2.5-flash-image`, but is blocked exactly by Gemini image-generation free-tier quota `limit: 0`, not by Runway, UI wiring, quota period, legal-safety false positive, missing/invalid key, API service enablement, or wrong model name.
goal_loop_10m_readiness_slice: `goal-loop-10m-20260626` reopened the 10M readiness loop and found a launch-ops/monitoring regression in the proof layer: the production app had moved to the Lightchain-style generation screen, but the monitoring UI probe still used stale Generate assumptions. The production monitor failed because its launch-ops child probe crashed on the old textarea locator; a later launch-ops rerun reached the page but exposed the stale mobile expectation `画像生成` after the current mobile shell now shows `HEAVYCHAIN`, `キャンペーン画像`, and `Runway workerで生成`. The fix adds accessible `Textarea` label association via `useId`/`htmlFor`, makes launch-ops target the real `ベースコンセプト` form field, requires the final `Runway workerで生成` button to be visible and enabled without clicking it, and updates mobile Generate expectations to the current shell. Local checks passed: `node --check scripts/verify-launch-operations-readiness.mjs`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`; Codex review findings were addressed in this slice. Commit `eae2d60` was pushed to `main`; Zeabur flipped to `assets/index.BxbR6FIC.js`; production launch-ops passed at `output/playwright/goal-loop-10m-20260626/launch-ops-after-deploy/summary.json` with `ok=true` and `failed=[]`; production monitor passed at `output/playwright/goal-loop-10m-20260626/production-monitor-after-deploy/summary.json` with `ok=true`, blockers `0`, generation failure rate `0`, stale active jobs `0`, storage errors `0`, and UI `ok=true`; Lightchain clone production proof passed again at `output/playwright/goal-loop-10m-20260626/lightchain-clone-prod-after-deploy/SUMMARY.json` with `ok=true` and `failed=[]`. The monitor still records two non-blocking historical/audit warnings, but no active production blocker remains for this slice. Pre-fix blockers remain preserved in `output/playwright/goal-loop-10m-20260626/launch-ops-rerun/summary.json` and `output/playwright/goal-loop-10m-20260626/production-monitor/summary.json`.

Post-push production QA found `/gallery` could remain as a spinner-only screen when remote generated-image or signed-url readback was slow, and `verify-mass-market-qa` still expected `/generate?feature=campaign-image` to enable the Runway button without the H601 rights checkbox. `GalleryPage` now renders the normal Gallery shell during loading, applies a 10s timeout to remote image and signed-url fetches, falls back to local workspace artifacts, shows a visible warning plus `再読み込み`, and preserves the empty-state `画像を生成する` path instead of trapping users on a spinner. The mass-market verifier now checks H601 rights copy and ticks the rights checkbox before asserting generate-ready without clicking submit. Verification passed locally: `node --check scripts/verify-mass-market-qa.mjs`, `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `git diff --check`, and `npm run verify:mass-market-qa -- --baseUrl http://127.0.0.1:4173 --out output/playwright/local-post-fix-mass-market-20260701-r1` (`ok=true`, `failed=[]`). Git proof: commit `48f01d2` pushed to `origin/main`; Zeabur served new asset `/assets/index.UhkYvOj9.js`. Production proof after deploy: `output/playwright/prod-post-gallery-fix-mass-market-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` (`ok=true`, `failed=[]`, zero console/page/request failures, 17 desktop + 8 mobile routes, videos, cleanup closed) and screenshot-inspected Gallery desktop/mobile pages showing warning, reload, empty state, and generate CTA instead of an infinite spinner. Lightchain regression proof also passed at `output/playwright/prod-post-gallery-fix-lightchain-beginner-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` (`ok=true`, `failed=[]`). G622 is now wired into both release gate and 10M completion audit; `output/playwright/release-gate-g622-current-20260701-r2/summary.json` reads current production mass-market QA as passed, and `output/playwright/10m-completion-audit-20260701-g622-r2/summary.json` records `g622_current_production_mass_market_qa` as passed while preserving the remaining G617/G619/H601/H602/domain blockers.

generation_material_workbench_slice: `lightchain-parity-generation-material-workbench-20260625` extends the image-first Lightchain pattern into the full `/generate?feature=...` matrix and hardens the local Runway worker handoff. The generate page now adds a visual material workbench for `campaign-image`, `design-gacha`, `product-shots`, `model-matrix`, `scene-coordinate`, `colorize`, `remove-bg`, `upscale`, `variations`, and `multilingual-banner`, with upload preview, material recognition state, cut/mask mode, layer selection, placement, scale, material memo, and Canvas structure preview before any generation request. Material metadata is propagated into prompts, local worker metadata, no-image planning metadata, and Edge Function request bodies. Supabase Edge Functions now sanitize material generation metadata via `_shared/materialMetadata.ts`, preserving `materialReferences`, `layerPlan`, `maskPlan`, and `compositionPreview` while stripping raw `imageUrl` values from persisted metadata. `generate-image` now stores local worker reference images as private Storage handoff objects and persists only `referenceImageHandoff` path/hash/bytes plus `hasReferenceImage` in `generation_jobs.input_params`; the local Runway worker downloads that handoff into memory for the generation call, writes redacted job/image artifacts, and removes the handoff object on completion/failure. Deployed commit `73bdbcc` to `main`; Supabase functions `remove-background`, `colorize`, `upscale`, `generate-variations`, `design-gacha`, `model-matrix`, `multilingual-banner`, and `product-shots` were deployed to project `ghwjymozrwmcrpjqvbmo`, then `generate-image` was redeployed with the Storage handoff redaction fix; Zeabur asset flipped to `assets/index.Bb_aqTCC.js`. Verification passed: `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `node --check scripts/local-runway-mcp-worker.mjs`, `npx deno check` for all changed Edge Functions, `git diff --check`, `npm run build`, authenticated local Playwright proof, authenticated Zeabur Playwright proof, production operation recording, and production DB/Storage readback proving pending queue input has no raw `referenceImage`, material `imageUrl` is removed, the handoff object is readable, and cleanup deleted the test job, usage event, and Storage object. Local proof: `output/playwright/lightchain-parity-generation-workbench-local-20260625/summary.json`; local video: `output/playwright/lightchain-parity-generation-workbench-local-20260625/videos/page@1df161d68db702f0d0370080f76df03b.webm`; production proof: `output/playwright/lightchain-parity-generation-workbench-prod-20260625/summary.json`; production video: `output/playwright/lightchain-parity-generation-workbench-prod-20260625/videos/page@486c2c973f40693340c413ee0ce42cae.webm`; redaction proof: `output/playwright/lightchain-parity-generation-workbench-prod-20260625/prod-redaction-readback.json`.

reference_image_stability_slice: `reference-image-stability-20260625` fixes the approved-client reference-image path. Directly passing Supabase signed URLs or hydrated `data:image` values to Runway `referenceImages` is no longer the operator path; a real Runway upload test proved the same `S__4235312(1).jpg` succeeds when uploaded first as a Runway-hosted asset, producing task `522911d5-5bbb-4e4b-a567-0af6cc30b997` and a clean black hoodie reference-based output. `npm run build:local-runway-handoff` now downloads the private Heavy Chain reference handoff into `output/.../references/<job-id>-<sha>.jpg|png|webp`, writes `referenceImage.runwayAssetRequired=true`, and prompts the approved operator to upload that local file to Runway before calling `generate_image`; it deliberately avoids emitting Supabase signed URLs, `data:image` payloads, local filesystem paths, or Heavy Chain Storage paths as Runway `referenceImages`. Release and completion gates now require the G660 direct-detail-route assertion. This improves Dashboard-to-Lightchain usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
next_action: Keep the NisenPrints-proven Runway path as the production operator flow: start `npm run worker:local-runway:watch`, generate through an approved existing Runway MCP client, save the resulting MCP JSON into `output/runway-mcp-results/inbox` with `heavyChainJobId` or `generationJobId`, and let watch mode move successful files to `processed/`. Single-job `--mcp-result` imports may leave the source JSON in place as an audit artifact. Untagged JSON is not consumed by default; only use `--allow-unmatched-mcp-result` for a deliberate one-off recovery. Do not use `localhost:15554` consent pages; that is the `mcp-remote` dynamic-client path and currently fails Runway consent. `--live-runway` remains diagnostic only, and direct Runway API or hosted bridge should not be used as a fallback.
blocker: No active production blocker remains for verified real-image generation path, 2026-06-26 Lightchain product-excellence UI slice, 2026-06-26 generation-polish slice, 2026-06-26 production monitoring loop, `lightchain-full-clone-workbench-and-all-feature-qa-20260626` UI shell, G618 scale/ops baseline, G620 security-operations baseline, G619 beta evidence acceptance gate, 2026-07-01 Lightchain beginner detail-route UX slice, Zeabur production H601 UI readback, or the 2026-07-01 Gallery loading fallback / mass-market route QA slice. However, G617 same-run all-10 fresh generation remains incomplete: the old Runway path hit connected workspace/model unavailability (`workspace_limit`) after a successful minimal probe, and the Gemini standard path now reaches Google Generative Language with a valid AI Studio key but returns `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` because Nano Banana / Nano Banana 2 / Nano Banana Lite / Nano Banana Pro image-generation free-tier quota is `limit: 0` for this project; Imagen 4 also requires a paid plan. The older `mcp-remote` localhost consent path remains invalid historical evidence, not current restart path. Next generation acceptance should use Gemini after eligible image-generation quota is available for the project behind the active key, then rerun marker-scoped live generation, DB/Storage/Gallery/Jobs/Canvas readback, visual scorecard, and cleanup; Runway can remain an explicit fallback only if the approved existing Runway MCP client can sustain tasks. G619 still needs real consented beta sessions. H601 local source guard covers UI/helper generation and edit paths plus shared server-side generation Edge Function entrypoints, `npm run verify:h601-legal-safety` passes, and Zeabur production H601 rights/caveat/checkbox readback passes; H601 final legal/operator decisions remain human-needed. The custom domain `https://heavy-chain.com` is currently unreachable with `ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED`; use `https://heavy-chain.zeabur.app` for verified production QA until DNS/hosting is fixed. Full 10M public-launch completion remains unproven until live Gemini-or-approved-Runway all-feature generation proof, G619, H601 final human decisions, H602 completion, public entrypoint/domain, and final release gates are closed.
risk_gate: Billing, purchase, payment, checkout, identity verification, security prompts, secret entry, external public publishing, and final legal-policy approval still require explicit human approval. Non-billing production UAT may click generation submit only after the upload-rights confirmation gate, create marker-scoped jobs/images/usage rows, verify DB/Storage/UI readback, and clean up only those marker-scoped test artifacts. Future storage/auth/RLS/deploy/external-data changes require fresh proof.
maturity_candidate: release_prep
record_replay_computer_use: Record & Replay / Computer Use may be used for stable production readback, visual parity checks, Runway bridge status inspection, and Codex app/desktop GUI steps when Playwright/API/file proof is insufficient. They must not replace root `STATE.md`, deployment/readback proof, DB/readback artifacts, `verify:*` commands, or cleanup proof. Deployment, secrets, storage/auth/RLS, external service settings, billing, purchase, payment, and checkout still require explicit approval or a fresh workflow contract.

lightchain_direct_tool_screens_20260701: G661 makes Lightchain tool cards open their dedicated `/lightchain/:toolId` screens directly rather than relying on an in-page side detail prompt. Local proof `output/playwright/local-g661-lightchain-direct-tool-links-20260701-r2/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, cleanup closed, and desktop/mobile direct-detail route assertions. Production proof is now adopted from the G662 broad production QA `output/playwright/prod-post-g662-gemini-provider-default-20260701-r3/SUMMARY.json`, which passed with `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, zero console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, `lightchain_tool_cards_open_detail_routes`, `mobile_lightchain_tool_cards_open_detail_routes`, and `mobile_dashboard_lightchain_cards_open_detail_routes`. This improves Lightchain feature-screen usability but does not close G617/G619/H601/H602 or the custom domain blocker.
gemini_provider_default_groundwork_20260701: G662 switches the standard source/config/docs path from Runway worker to Gemini for normal image generation. `generate-image` now accepts `generationProvider`, defaults to Gemini, calls `_shared/geminiImage.ts`, records provider metadata, returns image arrays, and only requires Runway MCP approval for explicit Runway/local-worker paths. `_shared/geminiImage.ts` uses server-side `GEMINI_API_KEY`, supports `GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL`, parses inline image responses, and fails closed as `gemini_api_key_missing` / `gemini_image_request_failed` / `gemini_image_empty_response` without exposing secrets. Frontend `/generate` defaults to `VITE_GENERATION_PROVIDER=gemini`, shows `Geminiで生成`, and sends `generationProvider: gemini`; docs/env examples and static gates now treat Gemini as the standard runtime provider while keeping Runway as explicit fallback/legacy. Proof passed: `npm run verify:gemini-provider`, `npm run smoke:edge`, `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `deno check supabase/functions/_shared/geminiImage.ts supabase/functions/generate-image/index.ts`, `git diff --check`, and local mass-market QA `output/playwright/local-g662-gemini-provider-default-20260701-r1/SUMMARY.json` with `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, and screenshot inspection of desktop/mobile `Geminiで生成`. Production readback after GitHub push passed: Zeabur serves `assets/index.CYduUJuB.js`, Generate chunk contains `Geminiで生成`, and production mass-market QA `output/playwright/prod-post-g662-gemini-provider-default-20260701-r3/SUMMARY.json` has `ok=true`, `failed=[]`, 17 desktop routes, 9 mobile routes, zero console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, and desktop/mobile Generate buttons show `Geminiで生成`; r1/r2 are not adopted because r1 had browser close timeout and r2 saw transient Canvas 503. API key was not provided in this session, so live Gemini generation, all-10 scorecard, and any G617 replacement proof remain unproven.

repeatable_provider_success_loop_g678_20260702: Reusable initial-response pattern promoted from the OpenAI mini success: direct provider 1-image probe without source-secret writes; set only server-side runtime secrets; deploy/confirm Edge function; run one Heavy Chain marker probe with DB/Storage/signed URL/cleanup; cover all features with the cheapest safe split when cost matters; polish only weak outputs; merge readback and scorecard; rerun production monitor/mass-market QA; update source-of-truth docs with exact accepted/blocker scope. real_cutout_layer_stack_g679_20260702: G679 closes the local fake-preview gap for crop/auto-mask/layer stacking. `src/lib/workspaceMaterialReferences.ts` now provides a browser-canvas cutout utility that creates an actual PNG data URL with alpha transparency, records crop bounds, and tags the mask engine. Shared `MaterialWorkbench` extraction stores `extractedImageUrl`, `cutoutBounds`, and `maskEngine` and previews the extracted transparent PNG rather than duplicating the source image. `LightchainWorkbenchPage` runs the same extraction when the user clicks extract/extract-next, clears stale cutouts on upload/mode/layer/candidate changes, and saves the processed transparent PNG to Canvas. `workspaceHandoff` writes extracted workspace materials as separate original-base and extracted-cutout Canvas objects when extracted material metadata exists. Local proof `output/playwright/lightchain-mask-layer-flow-20260702T063042Z/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true failed=[]`; it verifies PNG data URL, real alpha transparency, cutout bounds, mask engine, original-base/extracted-cutout/overlay stack, reset behavior, Canvas route/readback, and cleanup. This is local product proof only; production readback after push/deploy is still queued.
post_g679_required_hardening_g680_20260702: G680 closes the proactive post-G679 storage/readback hardening in production. `buildMaterialCutoutDataUrl` now treats extracted PNG storage as `bounded-local-canvas-data-url-v1`: it progressively shrinks the cutout and fails closed instead of returning a cutout above `750000` estimated bytes. Shared `MaterialWorkbench`, `/lightchain/:toolId`, and `workspaceHandoff` now preserve `cutoutOutputSize`, `cutoutDataUrlBytes`, `cutoutMaxDataUrlBytes`, and `cutoutStoragePolicy` in material metadata and Canvas object parameters, so the saved layer can be audited after handoff. `verify-lightchain-mask-layer-flow` now accepts current beginner-detail screen signals instead of stale `機能一覧`-only proof, verifies the bounded data URL plus storage policy metadata in Canvas readback, and checks that cutout preflight happens before Canvas project creation. Local proof `output/playwright/g680-local-lightchain-mask-layer-storage-r6/SUMMARY.json` passed with `ok=true failed=[]`. After push `dd4c3f7`, production proof `output/playwright/g680-prod-lightchain-mask-layer-storage-r2/SUMMARY.json` also passed on `https://heavy-chain.zeabur.app` with `ok=true failed=[]`, no console/page/request failures, cleanup closed, `cutoutDataUrlBytes=3353`, `cutoutMaxDataUrlBytes=750000`, `cutoutStoragePolicy=bounded-local-canvas-data-url-v1`, real alpha transparency (`transparent=11816`, `opaque=19291`), original-base/extracted-cutout/overlay Canvas stack, reset behavior, save-preflight-before-project-creation guard, 33 feature detail routes, and video. Production r1 failed only during deploy transition on stale chunk 404 and is retained as non-acceptance evidence. This closes G680 only; G617/G619/H601/H602/public-domain remain open blockers.
internal_beta_ux_consistency_g681_20260702: G681 implements and accepts the Heavy Chain internal-beta UI/UX consistency pass in production. Dashboard now puts `まず1つ作る` and three first-result CTAs first: `商品画像から作る`, `Canvasで編集する`, and `SNS/EC画像を探す`. Generate, Lightchain detail, Canvas handoff language, Gallery empty states, onboarding, workspace pages, and feedback form copy were aligned to the common flow `素材を入れる -> 調整する -> 生成/保存する -> 再利用する`. Feedback now asks for concrete社内beta friction categories: `どこを押すかわからない`, `生成結果が微妙`, `保存先がわからない`, `動作が遅い`, `その他`. New verifier `npm run verify:internal-ux` checks old user-facing labels, dashboard CTA coverage, provider-neutral generation action, material workbench language, feedback categories, and navigation label. fitting_cutout_and_flow_g683_20260702: G683 improves the reported AI fitting friction and white-shirt cutout failure mode. `workspaceMaterialReferences` now adds edge-connected background flood cutout for auto/plain/tops/garment candidates, guarded by background spread, background ratio, and protected-center ratio; the same protected center is also enforced during alpha application so a white garment body is not fully removed as background. `/fitting` now starts with a clear `画像を入れて作る` action, shows the flow as `1. 衣服画像を入れる -> 2. 条件を確認 -> 3. AI生成`, and supports `/fitting#fitting-material-workbench` hash scrolling into the material workbench. `/lightchain/ai-fitting` now warns that fitting should continue from the AI fitting page and its CTA links to `/fitting#fitting-material-workbench` instead of leaving the user in the generic material workbench. Local checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `git diff --check`, Playwright local authenticated UI proof `output/playwright/g683-fitting-ux-local-r7/summary.json` (`ok=true`), and read-only Codex review returned `No high/medium risk`. Exact original upload file `4518992437842_a (1).jpg` was not available locally, so the exact user-upload pixel result was not verified on the original source image; this is a product hardening/UI fix, not a G617/G619/H601/H602/public-domain closure.
fitting_simple_quality_gate_g684_20260702: G684 responds to live user feedback that AI fitting and cutout UX was still too complex and that sleeve/garment loss is unacceptable. `/lightchain/ai-fitting` now opens a simple fitting handoff screen instead of the generic material workbench, and `/fitting` hides advanced workflow/history/detail controls behind summaries so the visible path is `画像を入れる -> 自動で切り抜く -> 高精度AI切り抜き待ち`. Shared `MaterialWorkbench` simple mode now treats browser-canvas cutouts as preview-only, shows an explicit warning that sleeves/thin fabric may be lost, hides simple-mode next-step confirmation, and blocks `confirmNextStep` in simple mode. `FittingPage` now requires an extracted image, `extractedLayerReady`, `nextStepReady`, and a non-`browser-canvas-*` `maskEngine` before enabling or running model-matrix generation; `runGeneration` repeats the same guard so retry/programmatic paths cannot submit low-quality local cutouts. The local cutout algorithm no longer stacks geometric alpha over valid edge-connected background masks, reducing avoidable sleeve clipping, but it is still not accepted as final fitting input. Visible Playwright CLI proof on `http://127.0.0.1:4180/fitting#fitting-material-workbench` used `output/playwright/g684-live-fitting-operation-r1/shirt-input-from-user-screenshot.png`: upload worked, local cutout produced preview/warning, no `次へ進む` was available, and `AI生成` stayed disabled even after rights confirmation; screenshot `.playwright-cli/page-2026-07-02T13-17-53-073Z.png`. Checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `git diff --check`, and read-only Codex review found no high/medium route for browser-canvas cutouts to reach generation. Remaining product gap: production-grade cutout needs a high-precision AI/image-edit provider path, likely OpenAI image edit with transparent PNG support, before AI fitting can safely generate from difficult white/light garments. This is not G617/G619/H601/H602/public-domain closure.
fitting_free_ai_cutout_quality_gate_g685_20260702: G685 adds a no-paid-service browser AI cutout path for AI fitting. `MaterialWorkbench` simple mode now runs local `@bunnio/rembg-web` with bundled `public/models/isnet-general-use.onnx`, stores accepted cutouts as `browser-ai-isnet-general-use-v1`, and keeps browser-canvas cutouts preview-only. The ONNX session uses `numThreads: 1` to avoid SharedArrayBuffer/COOP-COEP requirements in normal deployment. The post-cutout gate rejects results that still contain a rectangular neutral background or insufficient transparency, showing the inline error `背景の四角い範囲が残っています。服だけを分離できる写真で再試行してください。`; rejected cutouts do not set `nextStepReady` and `AI生成` remains disabled. Visible Playwright CLI proof on `http://127.0.0.1:4185/fitting#fitting-material-workbench` used the user's screenshot-derived shirt input at `output/playwright/g684-live-fitting-operation-r1/shirt-input-from-user-screenshot.png`; the local AI model loaded in the visible browser, attempted cutout, correctly rejected the gray-rectangle residue, and screenshot proof is `output/playwright/g685-ai-cloth-cutout-visible-r1/06-visible-single-thread-quality-error.png`. Checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `git diff --check`, and read-only Codex re-review found no high/medium residual issue after the thread-count and unused-model fixes. Local proof used an expired/local auth shim, so Supabase current-brand readback showed 401 and paid/external AI generation was not clicked; this closes only the free local cutout/gating step, not G617/G619/H601/H602/public-domain.

ai_fitting_canvas_direct_ux_g689_20260703: G689 implements the follow-up UX fixes from the user's screen recordings/screenshots. Canvas model-matrix only sends `modelReferenceImageUrl` when a garment/product reference image is also present, so product-description-only generation is not blocked or routed into a model-reference API failure. Edit modal prompt/colorize/variations paths now apply the same local legal-safety text guard and rights/brand gates before API calls. Lightchain feature cards now route directly to their actual feature routes instead of `/lightchain/:toolId` intermediate pages; Lab copy/action text clarifies it as `ウェアデザインラボ`; Brand Settings removed the top `生成前に整えること` and `Runway MCP接続` blocks. ai_fitting_white_background_fallback_g692_20260703: Responded to the live user report that `/fitting` could not cut out a white shirt on a white/light background because `isnet-general-use.onnx` returned HTTP 404, leaving AI generation blocked. Root cause remains production model hosting/configuration, but the fitting UI now has a quality-gated browser fallback for flat white/background product photos when the ONNX model fails to load. The fallback only accepts edge-connected background-flood cutouts with real transparency and rejects full-frame/geometric fallback (`boundsRatio > 0.92` or non-flood engine), so weak cutouts do not unlock generation. White-garment detection was tightened so bright garments are less likely to be mistaken for bright backgrounds. Visible Playwright CLI proof on local preview `http://127.0.0.1:4193/fitting#fitting-material-workbench` forced `/models/isnet-general-use.onnx` to HTTP 404, uploaded `output/playwright/g684-live-fitting-operation-r1/shirt-input-from-user-screenshot.png`, reached `高精度AI切り抜き済みです`, checked rights, and confirmed `AI生成` became enabled without clicking generation. Checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, `npm run verify:rembg-model-deploy`, `npm run verify:internal-ux -- --out output/playwright/g692-white-background-cutout-internal-ux-r2`, and `git diff --check`. Read-only Codex review found one fallback-quality gap and it was fixed by requiring flood-cutout provenance; final Codex review re-ran typecheck/lint/build/diff/rembg/internal-ux and found no high/medium issue. This is a local implementation/proof slice; production deployment/readback still needs commit/push/deploy verification, and a CORS-enabled `VITE_REMBG_MODEL_BASE_URL` remains the preferred true high-precision model-hosting fix.

ai_fitting_white_background_fallback_g692_followup_20260703: Production readback after the first G692 deploy still failed because `@bunnio/rembg-web` deferred the `isnet-general-use.onnx` fetch until `remove()` / session prediction, so the earlier `newSession()` catch did not catch the real 404. The high-precision cutout path now also catches model download failures during `remove()`, resets the cached session, and falls back to the bounded white-background cutout only for quality-gated flood-cutout results. The white-shirt/screenshot-edge case was also tightened: background estimation now samples bright neutral border medians so black screenshot/browser edges do not poison the background color, bright-background flood fill treats dark edge-connected bands as background, and near-background feathering can reach alpha 0 so transparent halos do not keep the crop full-frame. Direct dev readback on `output/playwright/g684-live-fitting-operation-r1/shirt-input-from-user-screenshot.png` returned `engine=browser-canvas-background-flood-cutout-v2`, `boundsRatio=0.9154`, `dataUrlBytes=106427`, which passes the fallback gate. Checks passed before production deploy: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build` with expected asset `assets/index.BTtAiT0H.js`, `npm run verify:rembg-model-deploy`, `npm run verify:internal-ux -- --out output/playwright/g692-white-shirt-edge-fallback-internal-ux-r1`, `git diff --check`, and read-only Codex review with `npm run verify:internal-ux -- --out output/playwright/g692-codex-review-internal-ux-r1`; the review found no high/medium issue. Production deploy/readback is next and not yet claimed in this entry.

ai_fitting_model_matrix_generation_g693_20260703: Fixed the user-reported blocker where `/fitting` could cut out a garment but `AI生成` failed with `Edge Function returned a non-2xx status code`. Root cause: `supabase/functions/model-matrix` was still hard-wired to the old Runway MCP approval/generation path while the production generation stack had moved to OpenAI image generation; therefore cutout success did not guarantee fitting generation. `model-matrix` now uses OpenAI image edit when garment/model references are present, uses OpenAI text image generation only when no references are supplied, records provider metadata as `openai`, reserves usage per requested matrix cell, and no longer requires Runway MCP approval for this AI fitting path. `src/lib/imageApi.ts` now surfaces the Edge Function JSON error body instead of the generic Supabase SDK message. Production deploy/readback succeeded on `https://heavy-chain.zeabur.app`: focused 1x1 authenticated smoke created a completed `model-matrix` job, inserted one `generated_images` row with `model_used=gpt-image-1-mini`, downloaded the generated Storage object (`1877165` bytes), then cleaned up the test image row, job row, and Storage file. Proof: `output/playwright/g693-fitting-model-matrix-openai-r1/summary.json`. Earlier failed proof `output/playwright/g693-fitting-model-matrix-openai-r1/summary-r1-failed-input-fidelity.json` records the OpenAI edit-model compatibility blocker that was fixed by removing unsupported `input_fidelity` for `gpt-image-1-mini`. Checks passed: `npm run typecheck -- --pretty false`, `deno check supabase/functions/_shared/openaiImage.ts supabase/functions/model-matrix/index.ts`, `git diff --check`, `npm run lint -- --max-warnings=0`, `npm run build`, and `npm run verify:h601-legal-safety`. Reference image fetch is now guarded to image data URLs or same Supabase project http(s) URLs only, rejects redirects/local/private hosts, requires allowed image MIME types, and caps streamed reference bodies at 8MiB. Test generation only was performed and cleaned up; no billing, checkout, payment, identity verification, CAPTCHA/OTP, external publishing, or legal finalization was performed. Remaining launch blockers stay separate: G617 strict same-run proof, G619 real beta evidence, H601/H602 human decisions, and public-launch readiness.

G620/static Runway checks were corrected so `model-matrix` is checked as OpenAI-backed, not Runway-backed; the runbook now separates Runway approval from OpenAI fitting generation. Visible Playwright CLI proof with mocked `model-matrix` passed: upload, cutout, rights confirmation, generated preview, JPEG payload/readback, and repeated AI生成 click produced exactly one request (`output/playwright/g700-fitting-reference-normalization-local-r8-double-click/summary.json`). Retry proof passed: forced first `model-matrix` 400, then three retry clicks produced exactly one retry request and preview (`output/playwright/g700-fitting-retry-lock-local-r1/summary.json`). Local broad QA passed: `output/playwright/g700-local-mass-market-fitting-normalization-r4/SUMMARY.json` ok=true; internal UX `output/playwright/g700-internal-ux-fitting-reference-normalization-r3/summary.json` ok=true; checks passed typecheck, lint, build, Supabase static verification, `git diff --check`, and final read-only Codex review OK.

ai_fitting_model_matrix_prod_unblocked_g705_20260704: Fixed the production AI fitting generation failure without billing, checkout, payment, identity verification, CAPTCHA/OTP, external publishing, destructive cleanup, or quota bypass. Root cause was the `/fitting` / model-matrix default requesting 4 patterns (`slim,regular` x `20s,30s`) against the current 3-unit/minute user rate limit, causing `User usage rate limit exceeded`. The app now defaults Fitting/Generate/Canvas model-matrix entry points to one pattern (`regular` x `20s`), blocks >3 patterns in UI, and the `model-matrix` Edge Function rejects >3 requested units before usage reservation. Commit `23dca92` was pushed to `origin/main`; `model-matrix` Edge Function was deployed to Supabase project `ghwjymozrwmcrpjqvbmo`; production served expected asset `assets/index.egBvsFYj.js`. The visible Playwright CLI verifier was hardened to wait for the submitted response only, load `.env.production.local`, ignore placeholder Supabase URL/key values, read back DB/Storage via REST, download generated PNGs, and future submit-mode acceptance now requires explicit visual review tied to the downloaded artifact path or SHA. Existing visible production E2E proof `output/playwright/g705-visible-fitting-prod-submit-post-deploy-r8-accepted/summary.json` is the technical/UI proof: `technicalOk=true`, `responseStatus=200`, `jobStatus=completed`, one DB generated image, Storage PNG download `downloadedBytes=2402422`, plus video/screenshots. The final visual acceptance proof is separate: downloaded output `output/playwright/g705-visible-fitting-prod-submit-post-deploy-r8-accepted/downloaded-6f737093-c517-4f54-8eed-6fe73da3805b.png` has SHA `05a5b3462056b5d600cb1ba608bdbd8de3b417c0a99830dce0a94216c6078163`, and `output/playwright/g705-visible-fitting-prod-visual-review-r1/visual-review.json` binds the accepted visual review to that exact SHA/downloaded artifact. Visual review result: the white shirt is naturally worn, with no major collar/sleeve/front-placket/hem breakage.

## 2026-07-19 Heavy Chain cloth-model local compatibility

- No Chrome or recording operation was performed; the user owns the current screen recording. The pinned official `u2net_cloth_seg.onnx` was inspected and run locally through `onnxruntime-web` WASM.
- The model identity is 176,194,565 bytes with SHA-256 `6d2cbc27bfbdc989e1fd325656d65902ecc6a3ccbe94b2d3655ec114efcb128e`. Runtime metadata confirms input `[batch,3,768,768]` and output `[batch,4,768,768]`, representing background plus upper/lower/full garment classes.
- A model-worn screenshot crop completed with 50,675 upper pixels, 0 lower pixels, and 19,448 full-body pixels. Raw `session.run()` varied from about 25.4 to 48.4 seconds across runs. A low-resolution reference-video dress crop returned `cloth_model_returned_no_garment_pixels` and is retained as failure evidence rather than quality proof.
- The local diagnostic now creates category mask PNGs, rejects malformed crop input, invalidates stale success evidence before every run, and always writes structured failure JSON. Production cloth prediction has a dedicated 90-second `predict()` budget inside a cloth-only 105-second page budget, plus a single-flight guard so a non-cancellable timed-out ONNX run cannot overlap another cloth inference. Existing fallback returns to the confirmed tap mask/manual editor.
- Verification passed: compatibility tests `5/5`, garment policy `7/7`, print-mask wiring, typecheck, production build, targeted ESLint, and `git diff --check`. Independent review verdict: APPROVE. Summary: `/Users/nichikatanaka/Documents/New project/work/heavy-chain-visual-flow-20260717/implementation-20260719/cloth-model-local-compatibility.json`.
- Remaining exact blocker is `managed_cors_cloth_model_url_not_configured`. Browser Canvas parity, production CORS, semantic quality acceptance, and 0713 video equivalence remain unproven.

## 2026-07-19 Cloth-model host release gate

- Added a fail-closed host verifier for the optional 176,194,565-byte `u2net_cloth_seg.onnx`. It rejects credentials/query/fragment, non-HTTPS or non-public literal/DNS addresses, redirects, non-200 responses, CORS/type/size/hash mismatches, and binds the TLS connection to the validated DNS address set.
- `build:rembg-cloth-model` now recreates `output/rembg-cloth-model-host.json` first, then runs the required deployment preflight, build, and dist URL readback under the same environment. A stale standalone proof can no longer substitute for the release command.
- Host tests pass 10/10, compatibility tests 5/5, deployment tests 6/6, candidate wiring/typecheck/build/targeted ESLint/diff checks pass, and independent review returned `APPROVE`. Proof: `/Users/nichikatanaka/Documents/New project/work/heavy-chain-visual-flow-20260717/implementation-20260719/cloth-model-host-release-gate.json`.
- No managed public model URL exists yet, so no actual 176MB public GET was run. Exact blocker remains `managed_cors_cloth_model_url_not_configured`. This is local, uncommitted, and undeployed; semantic garment recognition, accepted experimental quality, and 0713 video equivalence remain unclaimed.
